### CR007-017 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن ما عداهما ممّا يتعلق بهذه النشأة الدنيوية كلّها أمانة وعارية واجب ردّها

**English:** All other possessions of this worldly life are a trust or loan required to be returned.

**Editorial decision:** The prior reference is faith and enablement of good works, continuing as eternal gifts.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-018 · revise

**Arabic:** فالفتح إبداء خير من مضيق خفائية وإظهار سعة ونور من مضيق ظلمة إجمال

**English:** Opening is the disclosure of a good from narrow hiddenness, breadth and light from the constriction of undifferentiated obscurity.

**Editorial decision:** Māniʿ here is not a spatial doorway alone: the governing contrast is metaphysical articulation from latent concentration.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-019 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم في فتح الصور المثالية من مادة عين الهباء

**English:** Next, the opening of imaginal forms from the very matter of primordial dust (habāʾ).

**Editorial decision:** Do not mistake imaginal for merely subjective fantasy.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-020 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم في فتح الصور العنصرية من عين مادة الهباء والعنصر الأعظم

**English:** Then, the opening of elemental forms from the primordial dust and the supreme element.

**Editorial decision:** A distinct stage from imaginal forms, preserving the author's hierarchical cosmology.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-021 · crux

**Arabic:** ثم في فتح مشيمة كل أنثى من الحيوان والإنسان لقبول النطفة بعد ضيقها وارتتاقها

**English:** Then, the opening of the generative membrane of each female animal or human to receive the seed after constriction and closure.

**Editorial decision:** مشيمة literally may designate placenta/afterbirth in later Arabic, but the author's preconception function suggests 'uterine membrane'; do not replace with anatomically modern 'placenta' without historical lexical study.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-022 · revise

**Arabic:** وأتمّه وأعمّه نفعا وحكما فتح باب تولّد القلب من مضيق مشيمة النفس

**English:** Most comprehensive is opening the birth of the heart from the constriction of the soul's enveloping membrane.

**Editorial decision:** Retain repeated مشيمة imagery as a deliberate analogy of a spiritual birth, not an accidental metaphor.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-023 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم أعلاه وأكمله وأولاه الفتح المبين وهو فتح التجلّي وكشف الأنوار الحقيّة

**English:** Highest of all is the manifest opening: divine self-disclosure and unveiling of the lights of the Real.

**Editorial decision:** The series culminates in maʿrifa, not merely intellectual discrimination.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-024 · retain

**Arabic:** فإمداد الصور الروحانية برزق العلوم والمشاهدات

**English:** Spiritual forms are sustained through sciences and acts of witnessing.

**Editorial decision:** The word rizq has a genuine analogical extension beyond physical food.

### CR007-025 · retain

**Arabic:** الذي يعطي كفاف كل موجود بما به قوامه من القوت والقوة بحيث لا ينقص ولا يفضل

**English:** The One who gives each being precisely its sufficient nourishment and strength, neither less nor more.

**Editorial decision:** Al-Muqīt is defined by fitting sufficiency (kafāf), not sheer abundance.

### CR007-026 · annotate

**Arabic:** فكان الغنى والمغني بهذا المعنى من توابع الجواد

**English:** Under this sense, self-sufficiency and enrichment are branches of the Generous.

**Editorial decision:** This classification is conditional upon the sense of possession and dispensing of resources.

### CR007-027 · revise

**Arabic:** وأما بمعنى عدم الاحتياج فهو من أسماء السلبية الذاتية لا الصفاتية

**English:** When it means absence of need, it belongs to the negative names of Essence, not the attributive names.

### CR007-031 · annotate

**Arabic:** حتى إن عين ذلك الاسم أو الحقيقة هو وحدة جمعية تلك الفروع

**English:** The very reality of a name or a reality is the comprehensive unity of its branches.

**Editorial decision:** A genuine whole–part/inclusion principle, not identity of linguistic strings.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-032 · retain

**Arabic:** بحيث إنه مهما غلب وظهر حكم واحد من هذه الأسماء والحقائق في تلك الوحدة خفي أثر اسم الجواد

**English:** Whenever one derivative name predominates in that unity, the effect of the Generous becomes hidden.

**Editorial decision:** Phenomenological dominance changes what appears without eliminating the inclusive source.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-033 · retain

**Arabic:** وحافظ وحدة جمعيتها إنما هو الاسم المقسط وفرعه الذي هو العدل

**English:** What preserves the unity of their inclusiveness is al-Muqsiṭ and its branch al-ʿAdl.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish conservation of a comprehensive unity from equal distribution of quantities.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-034 · annotate

**Arabic:** برعاية حفظ الوسطية فيها بين الوجوب والاستحالة والبرزخية بينهما

**English:** By preserving the middle position between necessity and impossibility and the intermediacy between them.

**Editorial decision:** Potentially relevant to contingent existence: mumkin sits between metaphysical impossibility and necessity.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-035 · revise

**Arabic:** فهذا الاسم اسم كلّي سار بجميع معانيه المذكورة في جميع الأسماء

**English:** This name [al-Rabb] is universal, pervading all the names in its multiple meanings.

**Editorial decision:** The antecedent is al-Rabb, not al-Muqsiṭ. The text begins a new parent category here.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-036 · revise

**Arabic:** وهو - أعني ذلك الاسم - مليكه وسيّده والقريب اللازم له وممدّه بالوجود مع الآنات بالخلق الجديد دائما

**English:** That name is his sovereign, master, enduring near presence, and supplier of being at successive instants through continual new creation.

### CR007-037 · retain

**English:** Lordship has two judgments: a general one and a particular one.

**Editorial decision:** This prevents misreading an individual's determining name as a separate lord.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-038 · retain

**Arabic:** من جهتي الحقيقة القابلة والوجود الظاهر فيها

**English:** In two respects: the receptive reality and the existence appearing within it.

**Editorial decision:** The name Allāh is universal relative to both intelligible receptive reality and manifested being.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-039 · annotate

**Arabic:** وأما الحكم الخاص الذي للربوبية هو ما ذكرنا أن كل ما تعيّن وجوده أولا من حضرة اسم كان ذلك الاسم ربّه الخاص

**English:** Particular lordship is that everything whose existence first becomes determined in a name's presence has that name as its proper Lord.

**Editorial decision:** The rule operates inside general Divine Lordship, not as polytheistic causal independence.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-040 · retain

**Arabic:** فيكون التجلّي الثاني من حيث ذلك الأثر الخفي الاختصاصي رب ذلك

**English:** The second self-disclosure, in respect of that hidden particularizing effect, becomes the Lord of that—

**Editorial decision:** Sentence crosses B2007 p66–67; preserve the referent completed by الكامل.

### CR007-041 · retain

**Arabic:** ومن قارب الكمّل قابلية واستعدادا وحيطة وكلية وذوقا وشهودا

**English:** Those approaching the perfected in receptivity, preparation, inclusiveness, universality, taste and witnessing.

**Editorial decision:** A graded, not binary, perfection hierarchy.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-042 · retain

**Arabic:** أو من أنهر فروع الفروع ، أو من جداول تلك الأنهر ، أو من السواقي

**English:** Or from rivers branching from their branches, then their streams and channels.

**Editorial decision:** Water-system analogy is hierarchical determination and return, not literal physical cosmography.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-043 · retain

**Arabic:** فبحسب الاستعداد يكون تعيّنهم أولا ومرجعهم آخرا

**English:** Their first determination and final return accord with their preparedness.

**Editorial decision:** Beginning and return are correlative within the same governing divine name.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-044 · annotate

**Arabic:** وأما نبيّنا محمد صلى اللّه عليه وسلم فله المنهل الأعلى ، وهو التجلّي الأول الذي هو نوره أولا ، وربّه ثانيا

**English:** Our Prophet Muḥammad has the supreme spring: the first self-disclosure, his light first and his Lord in a second regard.

**Editorial decision:** A technical Akbarī ontological claim, not an identification of a created human person with the Divine Essence.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-045 · retain

**Arabic:** وكلماته إنما هي الأسماء الذاتية المسمّاة بمفاتيح الغيب

**English:** Its words are the essential names called the Keys of the Unseen.

**Editorial decision:** These are not identical with ordinary uttered syllables; see earlier 'names of names' discussion.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-046 · retain

**Arabic:** فهي تنفد وتنتهي عند الرجوع إلى التجلّي الأول

**English:** They come to an end on returning to the first self-disclosure.

**Editorial decision:** Relative cessation of derivative determinations in ascent, not exhaustion of divine pre-eternal knowledge.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-047 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن الوجود الذي هو عين الرحمة التي وسعت كل شيء ، وهو عين الرحمن له جهتان

**English:** Existence, identical with all-encompassing mercy and the All-merciful, has two aspects.

**Editorial decision:** The two aspects concern bestowing and being bestowed, not two entities with rival essences.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-048 · retain

**Arabic:** إحداهما : أنه مفيض على الحقائق الكونية لإظهار الكمالات المتعلقة بها

**English:** In the first, it bestows upon cosmic realities, disclosing their perfections.

**Editorial decision:** Agentive consideration of existence as effusion.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-049 · revise

**Arabic:** وأما جهته الثانية : كونه مفاضا مضافا إلى الكائنات في المراتب الكونية

**English:** In the second, it is bestowed and predicated of creatures within their cosmic ranks.

**Editorial decision:** A precise difference between mūfiḍ and mufāḍ central to the user's ontology comparison.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-050 · retain

**Arabic:** فلا جرم كلمات اللّه في النزول والتعلّق بالأبدية لا تتناهى

**English:** Hence God's words are inexhaustible in descent and their relation to everlastingness.

**Editorial decision:** This and return's termination are not inconsistent because they refer to distinct directions.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-051 · annotate

**Arabic:** فإن حرف الهاء مع الواو يدلّ على الذات غالبا من حيث ظاهر حضرة الهوية

**English:** The letter hāʾ with wāw generally indicates Essence in the outward aspect of the Presence of Identity.

**Editorial decision:** Author's letter symbolism must not be presented as a universal rule of Arabic grammar.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-052 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن قلت : أن لو حرف امتناع شيء لامتناع غيره

**English:** If you object: law signifies the non-occurrence of one thing because another does not occur.

### CR007-053 · annotate

**Arabic:** لا نسلم بأن لو حرف امتناع لامتناع فحسب ، بل ووقوعه لوقوعه أيضا

**English:** We do not concede that law exclusively denotes non-occurrence from non-occurrence; it also denotes occurrence from occurrence.

**Editorial decision:** Attribution to Ibn Fāris is authorial and requires independent lexical verification before endorsing as received linguistic doctrine.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-007-p61-69.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR007-054 · retain

**Arabic:** ولكل واحد مفهوم خاص

**English:** Each of [the outward and inward readings] has its own distinct understanding.

**Editorial decision:** Links exoteric and esoteric interpretation without claiming textual license for arbitrary contradiction.

**English:** Under the outward sense, it indicates the impossibility of exhausting the ordinarily understood sea.

### CR008-001 · retain

**Arabic:** الأصل الثاني في ذكر أحكام مرتبة الأرواح وعالم الملكوت

**English:** The second foundation: the determinations of the spirits' rank and the world of Dominion (malakūt).

**Editorial decision:** The subject changes at p70; do not subsume its new causal explanation under the divine-name catalogue.

### CR008-002 · retain

**Arabic:** وكانت المحبّة الأصلية المعنيّة بأحببت وصلة بينهما

**English:** The primordial love meant by 'I loved' joins hiddenness and manifestation.

**Editorial decision:** The author's systematic link from the first to the second foundation.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-003 · annotate

**Arabic:** فبهذه المحبة الأصلية واقتضائها تعيّن وبدا وهي سارية فيه كامنة في باطنه

**English:** It became determined and appeared through primordial love, which pervades it while remaining concealed within.

**Editorial decision:** Love is not a chronologically later created impulse but an ontological explanatory principle in this system.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-004 · revise

**Arabic:** فإنه لا أثر للشيء في شيء إلّا بأمر باطن فيه أو منه

**English:** Nothing has an effect upon something else except through a reality inwardly present in it or issuing from it.

**Editorial decision:** This states a specific doctrine of inward causation, not a loose claim that every effect has an unseen cause.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-005 · retain

**Arabic:** لم يخل شيء من أثر المحبّة والعشق والطلب والشوق أصلا

**English:** Nothing is devoid of some effect of love, attachment, seeking and longing.

**Editorial decision:** Universal extension to cosmic forms is analogical and conditioned by their respective ranks.

### CR008-006 · retain

**Arabic:** فظهرت المفاتيح بحكم ذلك السريان من باطن كل اسم وصفة وحقيقة إلهيّة

**English:** The Keys of the Unseen become manifest from the inward aspect of every divine name, attribute and reality.

**Editorial decision:** Causes of activity appear through names, while recipient preparedness appears in cosmic realities.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-007 · retain

**Arabic:** فكل واحد من الحقائق يرجع بحكم هذا الطلب والشوق والسؤال الحالي والاستعدادي إلى أصله

**English:** Each reality returns, through desire and the petition of state and preparedness, to its own origin.

**Editorial decision:** A claim about existential dependence through preparedness, not necessarily a verbal supplication.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-008 · retain

**English:** Ultimately to the seven principles upon which the creative judgment and command depend.

**Editorial decision:** The seven principals remain the indispensable intelligible relations for creative disclosure.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-010 · retain

**Editorial decision:** Return follows ascent in the conceptual account, not necessarily time.

### CR008-019 · retain

**Arabic:** فالماء مثل الحقيقة القابلة والجدار الصقيل مثل المرتبة

**English:** The water signifies the receptive reality; the polished wall signifies the rank.

**Editorial decision:** Analogy of reflected light must preserve both the receiving reality and its locus.

### CR008-020 · annotate

**Arabic:** فهذه تمثيل مطابق من بعض الوجوه لا كلّها

**English:** This analogy holds in some respects, not in all.

**Editorial decision:** Author's own explicit restriction is essential to avoid reifying image reflections as the ontology.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-021 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم مد ظل تعيّنات الوجود المفاض على الكائنات والمضاف إليها في مرتبة الأرواح والمثال والحسّ

**English:** He then stretches the shadow of determinations of bestowed existence ascribed to creatures in spiritual, imaginal and sensible ranks.

**Editorial decision:** The successive 'shadows' are ontological dependence metaphors, not local displacement.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-022 · annotate

**Arabic:** فكان هذا المدّ منه على سبيل الإرادة والاختيار

**English:** This extension takes place through will and choice.

**Editorial decision:** Author explicitly rejects necessary emanation ('by essence') in favor of chosen disclosure.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-023 · retain

**Arabic:** وذلك القبض مثل رجوع الوجود كل آن إلى أصله

**English:** That withdrawal resembles existence returning to its source at every instant.

**Editorial decision:** Core passage for a rigorous comparison with theories of continuous creation.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-024 · crux

**Arabic:** لكونه عرضا على الحقيقة وقيام بدل مثله مكانه في الخلق الجديد

**English:** Because it is truly an accident, and its like stands in its place in renewed creation.

**Editorial decision:** The referent of عرضا may be the bestowed existence rather than absolute Being; investigate context and other print/MS witnesses before equating with Ashʿarite occasionalism.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-025 · retain

**Arabic:** على نحو رجوع الغذاء والدهن بالتحليل من البدن والسراج إلى ما بدا منه من الأركان

**English:** Like food and oil returning by dissolution from body and lamp to the elements from which they arose.

**Editorial decision:** An analogy of ongoing replacement with continuity at the manifest level, not a proof of substantial motion.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-037 · retain

**English:** The reality of the Pen receives bestowed existence only in an undifferentiated mode.

**English:** They are life, knowledge, will and power.

### CR008-039 · revise

**Arabic:** ففي إيجاد الأرواح كان القول ركنا ، والقدرة تتمّة له وشرطا وفي الأجسام بالعكس

**English:** For the origination of spirits, speech is a pillar and power its completion and condition; for bodies the reverse holds.

### CR008-041 · retain

### CR008-048 · retain

**Arabic:** والصنف الثاني : ما يضافون هم إلى المظاهر

**English:** The second subgroup is made dependent on their manifestations.

**Editorial decision:** Human spirits are described in this subgroup; opposite of angels whose manifestation is attributed to them.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-049 · annotate

**Arabic:** وهي سابقة على تعين مزاجه العنصري

**English:** The spirit precedes the determination of the person's elemental temperament.

**Editorial decision:** Ranked/ontological precedence; it does not assert the temporal preexistence of an individuated body-soul in the ordinary empirical sense.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-050 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن كل واحد من هؤلاء له قوتان ، ولا بدّ يطيرون بهما

**English:** Each messenger has two powers by which he necessarily flies.

**Editorial decision:** Metaphysical exegesis of wings; the literal hadith/narrative tradition is a distinct evidentiary layer.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-051 · retain

**Arabic:** إحداهما : قوّة علمية آخذه من موجده تعالى ، والثانية : قوة عملية عاملة بموجب ذلك العلم

**English:** One is a knowing power receiving from the Creator; the second a practical power acting according to knowledge.

**Editorial decision:** Knowledge and praxis are the two fundamental wings.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-052 · retain

**Arabic:** وهو تعليم غيره ما علّمه

**English:** A third wing consists in teaching another what one has learned.

**Editorial decision:** The pedagogical extension is an additional power beyond knowing and doing.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-053 · retain

**Arabic:** هو العمل لغيره بإذن ربّه وأمره

**English:** A fourth wing is acting for another by the Lord's permission and command.

**Editorial decision:** Intercession and service extend wing symbolism beyond the practitioner's own actions.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR008-054 · annotate

**Arabic:** فغير محصورة ، وما ورد في بعض الأخبار أن النبيّ صلى اللّه عليه وسلم رأى جبرائيل عليه السلام وله ستمائة جناح

**English:** Their particular powers are innumerable; reports describe the Prophet seeing Gabriel with six hundred wings.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish author's interpretation of a reported number from textual/hadith authentication.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-008-p70-78.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-001 · retain

**Arabic:** الأصل الثالث في ذكر تعين عالم المثال ومرتبة الأجسام إلى تكون صورة آدم عليه السلام

**English:** The third foundation concerns the determination of the imaginal world and the bodily rank, up to the formation of Adam's form.

**Editorial decision:** The author clearly distinguishes Foundation III from the noetic/spiritual register of Foundation II.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-002 · annotate

**Arabic:** كتفصيل نسبي لا حقيقي لذلك الإجمال الحقيقي بلا ظهور حكم غيرية ومغايرة

**English:** A relative, not real, differentiation of that true undifferentiated unity, without the appearance of otherness.

**Editorial decision:** Two senses of detailed unfolding must be kept apart: relational differentiation in the first rank and real differentiation in the second.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-003 · revise

**Arabic:** فجميع ذلك أضحى تفصيلا لهذا الإجمال ، ولكن تفصيلا حقيقيّا لا نسبيّا

**English:** All of that becomes differentiation of this comprehensiveness—real differentiation, not merely relative.

**Editorial decision:** Critical contrast with the previous first-rank condition; do not flatten both 'details' into identical factual plurality.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-008 · annotate

**Arabic:** هذا الهباء أصلا مجملا ومعدنا مشتملا على كل وجود فرد وجزء لا يتجزأ

**English:** This primordial dust serves as a comprehensive source containing every individual existent and indivisible part.

**Editorial decision:** The phrase جزء لا يتجزأ invokes indivisibility within medieval natural philosophy; do not identify it automatically with modern atomic physics.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-009 · retain

**English:** Its four principles are heat, cold, moisture and dryness, in their simple, not composite form.

**Editorial decision:** These are qualities/principles, not yet the four material elements.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

**English:** This dust comprehensively contains the differentiated Dominion of every thing.

**Editorial decision:** The relationship is structural within the author's cosmology.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-011 · retain

**Arabic:** كان اسم الطبيعة نتيجة ذلك الامتزاج ، فكانت الطبيعة برزخا جامعا بين الأركان المذكورة

**English:** The term Nature results from that blending; Nature is an isthmus encompassing the mentioned principles.

**Editorial decision:** Nature is an intermediate integrating principle rather than just a synonym for the four qualities.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-012 · annotate

**English:** In its expansion it assumed the form nearest to unity and simplicity, namely the circular shape.

**Editorial decision:** Cosmological geometry functions as symbolic and philosophical explanation, not a modern measured physical model.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-013 · retain

**Arabic:** حكم النزول إلى أنهى مرتبة الحسّ وتركيباته صورة ، وبهذا الحكم لزم وثبت الطول في الجسم

**English:** Descent to the extreme rank of sensory composition accounts for the body's length.

**Editorial decision:** First of three formal accounts of bodily dimension.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-014 · retain

**Arabic:** والحكم الثاني حكم التفصيل والتركيب الصوري ، وبذلك ظهر العرض فيه

**English:** The second judgment is differentiation and formal combination, by which breadth appears.

**Editorial decision:** Width arises by a different determination from length.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-015 · retain

**Arabic:** وبذلك تحقّق العمق في الجسم

**Editorial decision:** Third dimension tied to continued governing assistance from its originating source.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-016 · revise

**Arabic:** انقسمت صورة هذا العرش على اثنتي عشرة قسمة مفروضة لا محسوسة

**English:** The Throne's form is divided into twelve conceived, not sensed, divisions.

**Editorial decision:** A key qualifier: مفروضة لا محسوسة disallows presenting twelve actual sensed sections of the Throne.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-017 · annotate

**Arabic:** فكان هذا العرش مستوى الاسم الرحمن على جميع معاني الاستواء

**English:** Thus this Throne is the locus of the name the All-merciful under all the senses of istiwāʾ.

**Editorial decision:** Multiple conceptual meanings follow; the account is Farghānī's reading of scriptural language, not a single lexically mandated definition.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-018 · retain

**Arabic:** فهذا العرش هو أصل تعيّن صور الزمان بحركته الدورية

**English:** The Throne is the origin of the determination of temporal forms through its circular motion.

**Editorial decision:** Medieval astronomical model explains temporal measurement and must not be projected uncritically onto current cosmology.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-038 · revise

**Arabic:** وذلك بأن تكون هذه المظاهر والأسباب معدّات لقبول أثر هذه الأسماء

**English:** These manifestations and causes prepare the recipients to receive the effects of the names.

**Editorial decision:** The causes are described as مُعِدّات (preparatory conditions), not independently efficient natural agents.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-039 · annotate

**Arabic:** لا التأثير والفعل مضاف إلى هذه المظاهر والأسباب ، بل الفعل والتأثير مضاف إلى أعيان هذه الأسماء

**English:** Action and efficacy are not attributed to these mediating manifestations or causes, but to the realities of the names.

**Editorial decision:** Sharp causal doctrine: secondary causes as preparations, while direct creative efficacy is assigned to divine names.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-040 · retain

**Arabic:** تارة تفعل وتؤثّر هذه الأسماء بأعيانها لا بواسطة هذه المظاهر

**English:** Sometimes the names exercise their effects without mediation by those manifestations.

**Editorial decision:** Contrast usual mediated order with extraordinary direct operation.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-041 · annotate

**Arabic:** فانفتقت فكانت أرضا وصورها الاسم المصوّر صورة كروية من حيث الإدراك العقلي ومسطحة بحسب ظاهر الإدراك

**English:** It opened and became earth, fashioned by the Fashioner as spherical to rational apprehension but flat in the apparent view of the senses.

**Editorial decision:** Both descriptions are expressly qualified by different modes of knowing; no scientific endorsement of ancient cosmology is intended.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-042 · retain

**Arabic:** حتى صارت محكومة له أي للزمان

**English:** Thus things become subject to time.

**Editorial decision:** Time as the containing rank of bodily accidents under cosmological movement.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-043 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن كل ما يحل في محل معنوي أو صوري يكون تحت حكم ذلك المحل

**English:** Everything established in an intelligible or formal locus comes under the judgment of that locus.

**Editorial decision:** Recalls earlier account of the causal importance of the locus.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-044 · annotate

**Arabic:** يشبه أن يكون الزمان مظهرا وصورة لمرتبة الحسّ

**English:** It appears that time serves as manifestation and image of the sensory rank.

**Editorial decision:** Tentative يشبه أن prevents turning this into Farghānī's absolute dogmatic definition.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-045 · retain

**Arabic:** التي هي عين وحدته الحقيقية

**English:** Which is identical with His true unity.

**Editorial decision:** First rank of comprehensive unity prior to the second determination.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-046 · retain

**Arabic:** ولشدّة نسبة الكثرة إليها كانت متعلّقاتها ومحتوياتها مختصّة بالقبول والتأثّر والانفعال

**English:** Because multiplicity predominates, what it comprehends is characterized by reception and passivity.

**Editorial decision:** Concerns the Presence of possible known things, not the Necessity-presence.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-047 · annotate

**Arabic:** فكانت كلتا يديه يمين مباركة نظرا إلى الكمال الحقيقي لا النسبي

**English:** Both His hands are blessed right hands with respect to true, not relative, perfection.

**Editorial decision:** Theological symbolism is explained within the author's metaphysical language, not physical anatomy.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-048 · retain

**Arabic:** فالتوابع الثلاثة ههنا هي الأمزجة المتركّبة من هذه الأركان المسمّاة بالمولدات الثلاث التي هي المعدن والنبات والحيوان

**English:** The three dependent mixtures are the three generated kingdoms: mineral, plant and animal.

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī's graded physical–vital scheme; human is considered in a more comprehensive rank.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-049 · retain

**Arabic:** فأول ما تعيّن كانت رتبة الاعتدال المعدني

**English:** The first differentiated balance was the mineral rank.

**Editorial decision:** First of three material temperament balance levels.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-050 · annotate

**Arabic:** فقبل من حضرة الاسم المصوّر صورة معدنية ، ومن الاسم الحيّ أثرا يحفظ تركيبه

**English:** It receives a mineral form from the Fashioner and conserving life-effect from the Living.

**Editorial decision:** The noun 'life' used analogically of material conservation, not botanical sensation.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-051 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم تنزل الأمر الإلهيّ ... فتعيّنت رتبة الاعتدال النباتي

**English:** The divine command descends, and the vegetal rank of equilibrium becomes determined.

**Editorial decision:** Next hierarchy of natural composition in the author's framework.

**English:** Such as nutritive, growth-producing, generative, attractive, expulsive, digestive and retentive capacities.

**Arabic:** فتعيّنت مرتبة الاعتدال الحيواني والجزء الأصلي في تركيب المزاج الحاصل فيها إنما هو الجزء المائي

**English:** The animal equilibrium rank becomes determined, with water as the principal elemental component.

### CR009-057 · annotate

**Arabic:** وكما كانت الملائكة من جملة قوى اليد المضافة إلى الصورة الرحمنية ... كذلك كانوا ... مظاهر أوصاف حقيقة آدم

**English:** As angels are among the powers of the hand attributed to the All-merciful form, they also manifest qualities of Adam's comprehensive reality.

**Editorial decision:** Relational typology, not a claim angels are constituent biological organs.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-058 · retain

**Arabic:** وكمال كل إنما يظهر من جهتين وطريقين

**English:** The perfection of any whole appears by two ways.

**Editorial decision:** Perfection of whole by its comprehensiveness versus perfection through each part.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-059 · retain

**Arabic:** فبدأ بتكميل أجزائه ، وكان مبدأ ذلك التكميل خطاب الملائكة

**English:** He began by perfecting its parts, commencing with addressing the angels.

**Editorial decision:** Interpretation of Adamic creation narrative as educative perfection of the angels.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-060 · retain

**Arabic:** ومن أعظم شروط الخلافة العلم بجميع المراتب وبأهليها وبحقوقهم وأحكامهم

**English:** Among the greatest conditions of vicegerency is knowledge of all ranks, their inhabitants, rights and judgments.

**Editorial decision:** Links epistemic comprehensiveness to moral and administrative justice.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-061 · retain

**Arabic:** فإن الخلافة تقتضي التوسّط بين المستخلف والمستخلف عليهم

**English:** For vicegerency requires mediation between the one appointing and those governed.

**Editorial decision:** Mediation constitutes the office rather than sheer dominion.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-062 · crux

**Arabic:** فهذه ثمانية عشر خصلة ذميمة وخلّة سقيمة ظهرت منهم

**English:** These eighteen blameworthy characteristics were manifested from them.

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī's interpretation of angels in the Qurʾanic narrative is startling and must be clearly marked as his spiritual exegesis, not an established theological fact; verify count and context.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-063 · retain

**Arabic:** ثم الصلصالية بظهور أثر الجزء الناري وحكم خواصه فيه

**English:** Then the clay becomes ringing dry clay through the manifestation of the fiery element's effect.

**Editorial decision:** Part of staged composition imagery preceding Adamic completion.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-064 · annotate

**Arabic:** فلهذا المعنى أسند فعل النفخ إلى نفسه بصيغة الإفراد لا بصيغة الجمع

**English:** Thus He attributes the blowing of spirit to Himself in the singular, not the plural.

**Editorial decision:** A grammatical-exegetical argument that should remain distinguished from authorial Divine ontological doctrine.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-065 · revise

**Arabic:** فإن علم كنه الذات ممتنع ، وعلم الأسماء كلّها ممكن

**English:** Knowledge of the Essence as such is impossible, while knowledge of all the names is possible.

**Editorial decision:** Central epistemic thesis behind Adam's instruction in the names; distinguish 'knowing quiddity of Essence' from 'comprehending aspects of names'.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-066 · retain

**Arabic:** والألفاظ المركّبة من الحروف الدالّة على تلك التعيّنات الوجودية هي أسماء تلك الأسماء

**English:** Expressions composed of letters that signify those existential determinations are names of the names.

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī explicitly repeats the p44 linguistic/ontological distinction; words are signs of ontological names.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-067 · annotate

**Arabic:** فكأنه تعالى وتقدس عرّف وعلّم آدم حقيقة ذاته - أي ذات آدم

**English:** It is as though He made Adam know and taught him the reality of his own self—that is, Adam's own self.

**Editorial decision:** Phrase ذات آدم supplies explicit corrective gloss; must NOT misrender as knowledge of the quiddity of God's Essence.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-068 · retain

**Arabic:** فعرف بذلك التعليم والتعريف نفسه ، وبتلك المعرفة ربّه

**English:** Through that instruction he knew himself, and through knowing himself he knew his Lord.

**Editorial decision:** Derived from inward self-knowledge in this exegesis, with a maxim whose hadith authenticity requires separate treatment.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-069 · retain

**Arabic:** فلمّا بان عجزهم وتنصّلوا عما قالوا وما فعلوا عاد إلى تكميلهم بواسطة أصلهم وكلّهم

**English:** When their inability became manifest, He resumed their perfection through their source and encompassing whole.

**Editorial decision:** The angelic pedagogical arc is completed through Adam's instruction.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-070 · annotate

**Arabic:** فلمّا أنبأهم آدم بذلك حينئذ علموا وكملوا بذلك العلم من جهة كلّهم

**English:** When Adam informed them of this, they came to know and were perfected through that knowledge, in their relation to the whole.

**Editorial decision:** Page 100 describes the angels' learning through Adam, not their biological composition. Replaces earlier locus misassigned from p99.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-071 · retain

**Arabic:** حيث كانت الملائكة محصورين بحكم عالمهم ونشأتهم لم يهتدوا إلى معرفة ما كان خارجا عنها

**English:** The angels are confined to the rules of their sphere and creation, and cannot know what lies beyond it.

**Editorial decision:** Explains why a being with wider comprehensive perspective can teach angels.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-072 · retain

**Arabic:** فحجب بوهمه وأبعد بحجّته ولجاجه واعتراضه في مقابلة الأمر المطاع

**English:** He was veiled by his imagination and distanced by his argument, obstinacy and opposition to the command.

**Editorial decision:** Refers to Iblīs, not the angels generally.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-073 · annotate

**Arabic:** فإن أحدا لا يظهر شيئا إلّا بما فيه من ذلك بالفعل أو بالقوّة

**English:** No one manifests anything unless it already lies within him actually or potentially.

**Editorial decision:** Principle of manifestation invoked in Farghānī's reconstruction of the Iblīs episode.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

### CR009-074 · revise

**Arabic:** وهذا دليل واضح على أن الملائكة والأرواح والعقول لهم الترقّي والزيادة على خلاف ما زعمت الفلاسفة

**English:** This plainly proves, in Farghānī's view, that angels, spirits and intellects can progress and increase—contrary to what the philosophers claimed.

**Editorial decision:** Explicit anti-philosophers conclusion with an ontological/pedagogical argument. Avoid attributing one unified denial of progress to every philosopher without independent historical comparison.

**Data origin:** `data/critical-pass-009-p79-101.js` · `D-Forum-B2007-derivative` · historical-print verified: false; medieval manuscript verified: false.

## Exact restart and full critical-edition requirements

Review B2007 p102 onward (Fourth Foundation), with secondary-transcription grade if using forum copy; separately acquire photographed modern B2007 page images for pp61–145 and I1876 images, actual dated manuscripts C1107/R670/K273/F3966/Y706, establish continuous Arabic lemma text and folio concordances, and proofread all 128 introductory English page drafts against an explicitly chosen base. No definitive edition or manuscript variant is established by merging selected records.
